Model before rewriting:

find i: int(-3..-2, 3..4)
find j: int(0..4)
find m: matrix indexed by [int(-2, 3)] of int(10, 20)
find y: int(-3, 4, 10, 20)
find r: bool
find b: int(0..4)

such that

y = elementId(m, i),
r = elementId([3,1,2;int(1..3)], j) = 3,
b = elementId([toInt(false),toInt(true);int(1..2)], j),
elementId([10,20;int(-2, 3)], -2) = 10

elementId(m, i)
   ~~> element_id_to_identity_lookup ([("Base", 19000)])
catchUndef(m[i], i)

--

elementId([0,1;int(1..2)], j)
   ~~> element_id_to_identity_lookup ([("Base", 19000)])
